**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Bank Accounts

### addBankReconciliationAttachment

`bankAccounts.addBankReconciliationAttachment`

Add a reconciliation attachment. Zoho Books API v3.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zohobooks.api.bankAccounts.addBankReconciliationAttachment({
	pathParams: { account_id: '999999999999999', reconciliation_id: '999999999999999' },
	query: { organization_id: '999999999999999' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `pathParams` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `query` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="pathParams full type">

```ts
{
  account_id: string,
  reconciliation_id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  organization_id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `custom`


---

### createBankAccount

`bankAccounts.createBankAccount`

Create a bank account. Zoho Books API v3.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zohobooks.api.bankAccounts.createBankAccount({
	query: { organization_id: '999999999999999' },
	body: { account_name: 'Sample record', account_type: 'sample' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `pathParams` | `object` | No | — |
| `query` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `headers` | `object` | No | — |
| `body` | `object` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="pathParams full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="query full type">

```ts
{
  organization_id: string
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="body full type">

```ts
{
  account_name: string,
  account_type: string,
  account_number?: string,
  account_code?: string,
  currency_id?: string,
  currency_code?: string,
  description?: string,
  bank_name?: string,
  routing_number?: string,
  is_primary_account?: boolean,
  is_paypal_account?: boolean,
  paypal_type?: string,
  paypal_email_address?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `custom`


---

### createBankReconciliation

`bankAccounts.createBankReconciliation`

Create a bank reconciliation. Zoho Books API v3.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.zohobooks.api.bankAccounts.createBankReconciliation({
	pathParams: { account_id: '999999999999999' },
	query: { organization_id: '999999999999999' },
	body: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `pathParams` | `object` | Yes | — |

<Accordion title="headers full type">


<Accordion title="body full type">
